# Requirements for later execution

No product tests, Playwright adapter, XState actions, guards, context, invoked service or delays were added. Temporary parsers, simulators and graph replay validated the documents only. Their dependencies were installed outside the repository. Repository dependencies and original specs are unchanged.

| Check | Observed result |
| --- | --- |
| XState createMachine and shortest paths | 279 chart machines parsed; atlas parallel map parsed separately. |
| Reachability / intentional steady states | Every state reachable from its declared initial state; steady projected states explicitly document no visible exit. |
| Local journey replay | 2694 paths replayed to their declared ends; no disabled event found. |
| YAML versus JSON | Equal parsed configuration for each chart. |
| Mermaid syntax | 280 diagrams parsed; 0 syntax failures. |
| Published name closure | 13 exact names; each declares a producer and receiver; subscriber and per-state semantics remain review questions. |
| Quint witnesses | 42 context-local models × 24 traces × at most 100 steps accepted using seed 42; this is simulation, not exhaustive model checking. |
| Gherkin inventory | 231 features; 1,520 classified declarations; 1,636 concrete outline examples parsed. |
| Inventory closure | Each collected row has a chart reference or exclusion reason. Dynamic/unnamed UI and unscanned configuration forms may still be missing. |
| Cross-context composition | All 21 atlas journeys replay as 25 exact contextual legs, including the two added compositions. This is declarative path composition; no application data or event-delivery adapters were executed. |

## Validation versions and limitations

XState 5.33.2, @xstate/graph 3.0.4, Quint 0.33.0, Mermaid 12.1.0. Chart `machine.json` files contain only compatible declarative configuration and metadata. The standalone viewer embeds the Mermaid library and chart diagram data so its rendering has no CDN dependency. Its browser interaction and rendering were not exercised as product release evidence. XState [machine documentation](https://stately.ai/docs/machines) and [graph documentation](https://stately.ai/docs/graph) describe the validation APIs.

Whole-product helper modules do not all have independently mapped actions. A further 200 valid Custom Game traces using seed 43 supplied draw and invitation witnesses; four selected histories are retained as journeys. A connected quotient graph can still admit paths whose discarded hidden data are incompatible. Reuse a simulator witness to seed a lifecycle journey; do not assume arbitrary shortest paths satisfy Quint. Exact witness files are temporary, so reproduction uses the recorded seed, sample index, nondeterministic choices and SHA-256; trace proofs are not long-term archived release artifacts.

## Clock controls and deadline classes

| Rule | Required control / boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Guest rated puzzle allowance | Freeze UTC; seed 19, 20 and 21 attempted starts; advance across UTC midnight. Do not reset an unfinished attempt. |
| Billing trial / renewal | Provider sandbox clock; monthly/yearly plan and credit-covering versus card-needed renewal variants; seven-day offer trial. |
| Billing period / confirmation grace | Freeze injected clock at deadline minus 1 ms, exactly deadline and plus 1 ms. Code and system Quint retain access at equality; first later millisecond ends it. Reconnect with current live/ended/refunded facts. |
| Matching account proof | Ten-minute confirmation expiry; proof earlier than ten minutes and the expired class; provider token validity handled by sandbox. |
| Sign-in lifetime | Thirty days and revocation while offline; reconnect must not continue a dead sign-in. |
| Privacy recent proof | Strictly less than ten minutes; exactly ten minutes is no longer recent. |
| Export preparation / expiration | Prepare within twenty-four hours; seven-day ready lifetime expires at equality. Retrying a failed export retains the active account. |
| Ordinary data erasure | Advance thirty days after confirmed deletion; active accounts do not expire by inactivity. |
| Friend request decline | One-day wait mentioned but expiration action absent; owner must settle the exact resetting event before execution. |
| Multiplayer first moves | Thirty seconds for White, then thirty seconds for Black; normal clocks start only after both have moved. |
| Custom Game away claim | One minute continuously away; participant can claim, watcher cannot; no automatic resignation. |
| Puzzle auto-advance | Off / immediate / one second; only live solving → solved advances; restoration and wrong results do not. |
| Outbox sweep | One-minute cron is adapter housekeeping; drive pending work deterministically, not a user-visible minute wait. |

## System event sources and test modes

| Source | Events and needed setup | Test mode / open requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Auth providers | Verified versus unverified address; Apple relay; wrong account proof; provider failure; cancellation; revocation. | Mockingbird OAuth with canonical provider protocol; never stub the relying party. |
| Membership providers | Checkout/portal refused or opened; renewal declined/recovered; refund; cancellation completion; stale repeated callback. | Mockingbird Stripe; confirm Paddle support separately. No invented card number is promised. |
| Identity | SignedIn, SignedOut, UsernameChanged, AccountDeletionConfirmed. | Harness mutations/public domain actions; seed owner, recent proof and observer devices. |
| Billing | PaidAccessStarted and PaidAccessEnded. | Apply real provider event through the provider port and verify the resulting permission decision. |
| Spaces | SpaceOpened, PersonJoined, PersonLeft, SpaceEnded. | Room/sync harness; host, guest, departed and offline observer variants. |
| Friends | BecameFriends and FriendshipEnded. | Directional request, block and decline fixtures; hidden blocked person is never told the cause. |
| Matchmaking | MatchFormed. | Complete same-pool group, mutually acceptable range, availability and repeated-match fixtures. |
| Data/room recovery | Connection changes; delayed accepted results; malformed/stale input; reopen after interrupted write. | Virtual clock + injected ports; preserve literal failing inputs when bugs are actually found. |
| Export / deletion jobs | Preparing → Ready / ExportFailed; expiration; cancellation pending → complete; erasure deadline. | Drive outbox consumers with injected ports; retention and proof boundaries remain account-owned. |
| Reports / evidence | Available part, missing part, another edition’s part, unavailable report and recovery. | ObjectStore fixtures scoped by edition; no future image can replace an earlier recorded picture. |
| Feedback | Unreachable reporting, partial attachments, unavailable diagnostics, recording without video. | Injected reporter/recording ports; diagnostics deadline must not block keeping writing. |
